country_event = {
	id = auh_admin_crisis.1
	title = auh_admin_crisis.1.t
	desc = auh_admin_crisis.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_parliament
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = auh_admin_crisis.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 35 }
		add_political_power = -25
		add_stability = 0.03
		add_to_variable = { var = CZE_support value = 3 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = CZE_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SLV_support value = 2 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = SLV_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_ac_1a_tt
	}

	option = {
		name = auh_admin_crisis.1.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 35 }
		add_political_power = 25
		add_stability = -0.03
		add_to_variable = { var = CZE_support value = -3 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = CZE_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = SLV_support value = -2 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = SLV_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_ac_1b_tt
	}

	option = {
		name = auh_admin_crisis.1.c
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
		add_political_power = -50
		add_stability = 0.02
		set_country_flag = AUH_ac_nationality_council_promised
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_ac_1c_tt
	}
}

			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = AUH_h_two_armies_in_one }
			}
			add_ideas = AUH_arm_two_armies_in_one
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_arm_the_concession_already_stands_tt
		}
		add_manpower = 15000
		add_to_variable = { var = HUN_support value = 15 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = HUN_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_arm_the_language_of_service_tt
		set_country_flag = AUH_arm_the_language_was_the_price
	}
}

					}
					NOT = {
						has_idea = AUH_m_the_realm_that_is_only_a_garrison
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = auh_dnc.423 days = 1 }
			}
			country_event = { id = auh_dnc.401 days = 365 }
		}
	}
	option = { name = auh_dnc.401.a }

			NOT = { has_country_flag = AUH_h_advisor_apponyi }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = auh_dnc.323.apponyi.d
		trigger = { has_country_flag = AUH_h_advisor_apponyi }
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_parliament
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = no
	trigger = { tag = AUH }
	option = {
		name = auh_dnc.323.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = AUH_h_advisor_apponyi
				has_character = AUH_albert_apponyi
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = AUH_kalman_szell }
				remove_advisor_role = {
					character = AUH_kalman_szell
					slot = political_advisor
				}
			}
			add_advisor_role = {
				character = AUH_albert_apponyi
				advisor = {
					slot = political_advisor
					cost = 150
					idea_token = AUH_albert_apponyi
					traits = { ideological_crusader education_reformer }
				}
				activate = no
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_dnc_323_apponyi_tt
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_character = AUH_kalman_szell }
			add_advisor_role = {
				character = AUH_kalman_szell
				advisor = {
					slot = political_advisor
					cost = 100
					idea_token = AUH_kalman_szell
					traits = { smooth_talking_charmer brilliant_negotiator }
				}
				activate = no
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_dnc_323_szell_tt
		}
	}

			add_political_power = AUH_bill
			set_variable = { AUH_next_term = 120 }
			set_variable = { AUH_bill_carried = 0 }
			random = {
				chance = AUH_renewal_odds
				set_variable = { AUH_bill_carried = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = AUH_bill_carried
						value = 1
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = auh_ausgleich.2 hours = 4 }
			}
			else = {
				country_event = { id = auh_ausgleich.3 hours = 4 }
			}
		}
	}

			}

		subtract_from_variable = { AUS_support = 4 }
		subtract_from_variable = { CZE_support = 3 }

		}
	}

		add_stability = -0.10
		add_war_support = 0.05
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = AUH_quote_frozen
						value = 1
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { AUH_quote = 2 }
		}
		subtract_from_variable = { AUH_gemeinsamkeit = 6 }
		subtract_from_variable = { HUN_support = 12 }

country_event = {
	id = auh_clock.33
	title = auh_clock.33.t
	desc = auh_clock.33.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_hungary_parliament
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = HUN_support
						value = 25
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = auh_clock.35 days = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = AUH_county_resistance
						value = 50
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = AUH_clock_word_refused
						has_country_flag = AUH_clock_house_dissolved
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = auh_clock.35 days = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = HUN_support
						value = 50
						compare = greater_than
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = AUH_belvedere_dossier
						value = 25
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = auh_clock.34 days = 4 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = AUH_belvedere_dossier
						value = 50
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = AUH_county_resistance
						value = 50
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = auh_clock.34 days = 4 }
			}
			else = {
				country_event = { id = auh_clock.36 days = 4 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = auh_clock.33.a
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		add_political_power = 25
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_clock_budapest_weighs_tt
	}
}

		name = auh_clock.196.a
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_war_with = HUN }
			white_peace = { tag = HUN }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = HUN }
			annex_country = {
				target = HUN
				transfer_troops = no
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 100
		add_stability = 0.05
		add_war_support = -0.05
		subtract_from_variable = { var = HUN_support value = 15 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = HUN_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_m_tt_hun_down
		subtract_from_variable = { var = AUH_gemeinsamkeit value = 4 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = AUH_gemeinsamkeit
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_a_gemeinsamkeit_down_tt
		add_to_variable = { var = AUS_support value = 3 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = AUS_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_m_tt_aus_up
		add_to_variable = { var = CZE_support value = 2 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = CZE_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_m_tt_cze_up
		add_to_variable = { var = CRO_support value = 3 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = CRO_support
			min = 0
			max = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_m_tt_cro_up

		}

				OR = {
					state = 43
					state = 154
					state = 155
					state = 808
					state = 973
					state = 994
				}
				NOT = { has_state_flag = AUH_m_county_in_resistance }
			}
			set_state_flag = AUH_m_county_in_resistance
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = AUH_m_the_county_under_commission }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = AUH_x_commissioners_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { AUH_county_resistance = 5 }
		}
